## Toy Car to Real Car AI Trend: Your Car Drives Off the Train Window

- Page: https://www.makeviral.ai/templates/toy-car-to-real-car-ai-trend

- Local: [`makeviral/toy-car-to-real-car-ai-trend/PROMPTS.md`](makeviral/toy-car-to-real-car-ai-trend/PROMPTS.md)

### 1. The video prompt is filled in. Settings: Seedance 2.5, 10 s, 720p, 9:16, sound off. The video prompt

```text
Recreate the reference video [Video 1] shot for shot: a handheld phone camera inside a moving train films a tiny toy model car on the window ledge, the train goes into a tunnel, and the toy comes alive and turns into the real car, with the same framing, camera moves, light changes, cut and timing. The ONLY change: the blue sports sedan, both the toy on the ledge and the real car, is now the car in [Image 1]. The train, the window, the hills, the tunnel, the road, the lights and the camera stay exactly as in [Video 1].

The car = the car in [Image 1], in EVERY frame, both as the toy and as the real car: the same body shape, proportions, paint color, wheels, rims, windows and lights as in [Image 1], whatever kind of car it is (a sedan, a hatchback, an SUV, a pickup or a sports car), so its owner knows it at once. Never the blue sedan from [Video 1]: no blue paint unless [Image 1] shows it. As the toy it is a perfect small scale model of that same car, about the size of a hand, with the same colors. Take only the car from [Image 1], not its background, its people or its light. No badges, emblems or lettering on the car, and no readable number plate.

The moves, matching the reference second by second:
0-1.2 s: warm sunset light, green hills and a concrete wall slide past outside the train window; the toy model of the car stands still on the window ledge; the train goes into a tunnel and the window turns dark.
1.2-3 s: in the dark tunnel, lights streak past outside the window; the toy stands on the ledge.
3-6 s: the toy car's tail lights switch on and glow red on the ledge.
6-8.5 s: the camera glides out through the window: the window frame slides away, the ledge becomes the tunnel road, and the toy grows into the real full-size car that drives on through the tunnel, its red tail lights reflected on the asphalt.
8.5-9.1 s: the car drives out of the tunnel into a burst of white light.
9.1-10 s: the real car drives on a highway at night under tall street lights, filmed from behind and to the side.

Exactly one car; no people, no hands, no other vehicles close by. No badges, emblems, brand names or lettering on the car, no readable number plate, no text, no captions, no logos, no watermark.
```

```text
Recreate the reference video [Video 1] shot for shot: grainy black-and-white security camera footage from a fixed camera high in the corner of a small ATM booth at night, a grey steel ATM on the right wall, a dark glass door on the left, a bare floor, with the same wide angle, framing and timing, one continuous shot with no cuts; the camera never moves. The ONLY change: the woman with long dark hair, glasses and a long fur coat at the ATM is now the person in [Image 1]. The ATM, the booth, the cash, the light and the camera stay exactly as in [Video 1].

The person at the ATM = the person in [Image 1], in EVERY frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles) exactly as in the photo, in black and white like the rest of the footage. The same real person from the first frame to the last, so their friends instantly recognize them, also inside the storm of flying cash, when they sit on the floor and when they look up at the camera at the end. Never morph, never drift back to the original woman's face. NO glasses of any kind unless [Image 1] shows glasses. No long dark hair and no fur coat unless [Image 1] shows them.

The moves, matching the reference second by second:
0-0.6 s: the person stands at the ATM, half turned away from the camera, and takes a few bills from the cash slot.
0.6-1.2 s: a huge burst of banknotes explodes out of the cash slot, hits them and knocks them back toward the camera.
1.2-4.5 s: a thick, endless stream of bills keeps pouring out of the slot across the frame, a blur of flying cash that almost hides the person, and the floor fills with a carpet of banknotes.
4.5-6.5 s: the stream thins; the person sits on the cash-covered floor in front of the ATM with both arms full of bills, while bills still spill from the slot.
6.5-8 s: they hold a fan of bills up to the face, laugh, tip the head back and look up at the security camera with a big happy grin, the face clearly visible.

Anatomy rules: one head only, on the person's own shoulders; two arms, five fingers on each hand. Exactly one person in the booth; no other people. Plain banknotes with no readable text. No bank name or logo on the ATM, no readable screen, no timestamp or camera overlay, no text, no captions, no logos, no watermark.
```

```text
A phone video filmed from the audience at a glamorous televised fashion show: one continuous handheld take that slowly zooms in. A long glossy black runway with a glowing blue edge, a dark arena full of cheering people, small blue spotlights, white beams, camera rigs and photographers in the foreground.
THE MODEL: the person in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles), so their friends recognize them at once. [00:06-00:08] the camera zooms in closer: THE MODEL reaches the end of the runway, puts one hand on the hip and reaches the other hand out to the cheering crowd.

SOUND: an upbeat, glossy runway pop instrumental with NO singing and NO lyrics, a roaring, cheering crowd, whistles and camera shutters clicking; a soft kiss sound at the end.

The outfit covers the body like an evening gown: never a bra top, never underwear or lingerie as the outfit. No letters, words or logos on the stage, the screens or the clothes. ### 1. The video prompt is filled in. Settings: Seedance 2.5, 12 s, 720p, 9:16, sound on. The video prompt — Human

```text
A money flex clip in one continuous take with no cuts, filmed by a phone propped low on a dresser in a bedroom, looking slightly up at the person, like a real phone video. Photorealistic, vertical.

The performer is the person in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles) and the clothes from [Image 1], so their friends recognize them at once. The face stays the same from the first frame to the last; never morph, also behind the cash. If [Image 1] shows an animal, the performer is that animal standing upright on its hind legs behind the dresser, with its own face, fur and markings, holding the cash in its front paws, not a person.

The place: a simple bedroom in soft daylight: pale blue-grey walls, a made bed with a white knitted blanket behind, two small table lamps on the nightstands, a few round woven decorations on the wall, and a white ceiling fan with a light turning slowly above; in front, the dark wooden top of a low dresser close to the lens. The cash: generic banded bundles of green hundred-dollar bills, like movie prop money, joined end to end into a long accordion chain, plus two loose bundles on the dresser in front of the lens. The bills stay soft and a little out of focus: no readable serial numbers, no portrait on a bill in focus, no other writing.

The moves, in this order:
0-2 s: the person in [Image 1] stands behind the dresser and hugs a large armful of banded stacks toward the camera, the long chain of bundles folded in both arms, a smug stare into the lens.
2-4 s: they push the stacks at the lens, one hand swiping along the chain so the edges of the bills flick close to the camera.
4-5.5 s: they drape the long chain of bundles over one shoulder, so it hangs down to the dresser.
5.5-7 s: they toss the pile at the camera; the bundles fly at the lens and land on the dresser, covering its front edge.
7-9 s: they pick up a single bundle, pass it between their hands and rip off the paper band.
9-12 s: they spread the bills into a wide fan and end peeking over a fan of bills that fills the frame, eyes on the lens, a cocky little smile, the fan swaying slowly from side to side. The eyes, the eyebrows and the hair above the fan stay exactly those of [Image 1].

It is a playful show-off joke for a meme: a smug, deadpan flex, quick and confident moves, daylight, an ordinary bedroom. Nothing else in the hands: no jewelry close-ups, no other props, no luxury brand logos.

Audio: a heavy, bass-driven original trap beat with no lyrics, and the flutter and slap of the bills. The person never speaks. Exactly one performer, no other people, two hands with five fingers each, no extra limbs. No text, no subtitles, no logos, no watermark.
```

```text
A funny hand pinch filter clip in one continuous selfie shot with no cuts, filmed with the front camera of a phone held at arm's length, in a close-up from the chest up. Photorealistic, soft indoor light, an ordinary home room (a sofa or a plain wall) behind.

The person in the clip is the person in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles) and the clothes from [Image 1], so their friends recognize them at once. If [Image 1] shows an animal, that animal is the one in the selfie, with its own face, fur and markings, not a person.

0-3 s: the person sits still with their eyes closed and a calm, curious face, as if waiting to feel something, and says softly: "Apparently, if you close your eyes, you can actually feel it."
3-4 s: a big, realistic adult human hand with natural skin and short clean nails; only the hand and the wrist come into the frame, never an arm, a shoulder or a second person, enters from the left side of the frame and takes hold of the person's cheek between the thumb and the index finger.
4-7 s: the hand pinches the cheek and pulls it out to the side, gently shaking it; the cheek stretches out like soft, rubbery dough, further than real skin, in a funny, cartoonish way, with no pain. The eyes stay shut, the eyebrows go up.
7-10 s: the eyes snap open, the person flinches, stares at the hand in disbelief, then bursts out laughing and says: "No way!" The hand lets go, the cheek springs back to normal, and the hand slides out of the frame. The person touches their cheek and laughs into the camera.

The whole face stays large and sharp in the frame. The pinch is playful: no pain, no tears, no red mark, no blood. Audio: the room, the soft line at the start, a small surprised gasp, the "No way!" and real laughter. No music. Exactly one person; one hand only, no arm. No cartoon hand, no blue hand. No text, no subtitles, no captions, no logos, no watermark.
```

### 1. The video prompt is filled in. Settings: Seedance 2.5, 12 s, 720p, 9:16, sound on. The video prompt — Human

```text
Shot from the driver's seat at sunset. A hand passes a dollar out of the window to the person in [Image 1]. They take it, grin, step back and dance on the beat. One continuous handheld phone shot from inside the car, like a real iPhone video, photorealistic, vertical.

The person is the one in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles) and the clothes from [Image 1], so their friends recognize them at once. The face stays the same from the first frame to the last; never morph. If [Image 1] shows an animal, the performer is that animal standing upright on its hind legs, with its own face, fur and markings, not a person.

The place: a quiet street corner at a red traffic light at sunset: a gravel strip by the curb, a grey light pole, palm trees and round green bushes, a low wall and a parked car far behind, a warm orange and lilac sky. The car door, the open window frame and the side mirror show at the edges of the frame; no badge on the car.

0-1.5 s: through the windshield, the person stands on the corner by the light pole a few steps away. The camera pans to the open driver's window.
1.5-3 s: the person walks up to the open window. The driver's hand reaches out of the window holding one green one-dollar bill.
3-6 s: the person takes the bill, leans in close with a big surprised grin, looks into the lens and laughs: "No way, thank you!" The whole face is large and sharp: exactly the face from [Image 1].
6-7.5 s: they point at the camera, turn and step back three steps onto the gravel, then turn to face the car.
7.5-12 s: the beat drops and they dance a loose, bouncy side-to-side strut on the beat: knees bent wide, weight rocking from one foot to the other, shoulders rolling, arms swinging low and crossing at the chest, the bill in one hand, a cocky grin. The whole body is in view, feet on the gravel; the camera stays in the driver's seat.

It is a friendly tip at a red light, like a fun dare: golden daylight, an open street, the driver's hand and the bill always in full view, everyone smiling. Only ONE green one-dollar bill changes hands: no stack of cash, no bags, no packages, no envelopes, nothing else.

Audio: an upbeat original trap beat with heavy bass that drops when the dance starts, the car's idle hum, the person's happy "No way, thank you!". Exactly one performer outside the car, no other people, no extra limbs, two hands with five fingers each. No text, no subtitles, no logos, no watermark.
```

Main subject: [Image 1] is the sole protagonist: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles), so their friends recognize them at once; consistent throughout, never morph. If [Image 1] shows an animal, the protagonist is that animal.
Bystanders: Three to four friends stand on the left side of the rooftop, filming with phones.

CAMERA CONTINUITY: One-shot by a friend on the rooftop with a phone, no cuts.

STUNT ENGINE: Rooftop run-up → Leap over parapet → High-altitude descent → Hit the center of the elastic surface → Deep depression → Strong rebound → Mid-air tuck and flip → Feet return to the same rooftop → Cheering celebration.

SHOT 1 (00:00-00:10)
00:00-00:01.50 | Run-up: The protagonist glances back at the camera with an excited grin, the face large and sharp, then runs to the edge.
00:01.50-00:02.30 | Leap: They leap over the low parapet, arms spread; the filmer rushes to the parapet and tilts down.
00:02.30-00:04.80 | High-altitude descent: Almost vertically top-down, the protagonist falls fast, arms spread, accurately targeting the center of the device.
00:04.80-00:05.50 | Hitting the center: The black elastic surface clearly caves in, then rebounds with great force, propelling the protagonist vertically upward.
00:05.50-00:08.20 | Rebound and flip: They rise up the same vertical path, the camera tilting up, one tuck flip near roof height.
00:08.20-00:09.40 | Landing: Both feet land on the green pad, knees deeply bent.
00:09.40-00:10.00 | Celebration: The protagonist cheers into the camera, the face large and sharp; friends rush in.

Constraints: A professionally staged fictional extreme sports scene. The device clearly catches and rebounds the protagonist, never an unprotected fall; descent and ascent on the same vertical line, not flying or ropes.

Negative: face drift, character replacement, redesign clothing, mid-cut, camera teleportation, levitation, no elastic device, wrong landing point, device breaking, distorted limbs during flips, extra arms, extra legs, duplicate characters, falling and injured, blood, game CG, subtitles, screen text, watermark, brand logo
```

A viral AI hair dye video: the person from [Image 1] dyes their own hair burgundy from a bowl, then shows it off sleek and straight, then curled, filmed like a real phone video. Photorealistic, natural daylight, fine phone-camera grain, vertical 9:16.

The person is the one in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, and any beard, moustache, glasses, piercings, freckles or face marks), and the clothes and any jewelry from [Image 1]. The same face from the first frame to the last, so their friends recognize them at once, also when the head tilts down and the hair falls around the face. THE HAIR. Before the dye: exactly the hair from [Image 1], with the same color, texture, length and style (braids, a bun, curls or short hair stay as they are). After the dye: long, straight, sleek, glossy burgundy hair: a deep wine red with a brighter cherry-red shine at the roots and along a neat middle part, falling well past the shoulders. 3-4.5 s: a quick cut: the dye is done and the hair is now the long, straight, sleek burgundy hair described above. The bowl and the brush are gone; they run both hands through it.
4.5-6.5 s: a flat iron glides slowly down the long straight hair from the roots to the ends, with a thin wisp of steam, and the hair shines.
6.5-8 s: a quick cut: the hair is now in big, bouncy burgundy curls; they lift the curls with both hands and let them drop.
8-10 s: they flip the hair back over one shoulder, lift the chin and smile proudly into the camera, the glossy curls framing the face.

Audio: soft brush strokes, the hiss of the flat iron, a small happy laugh and a light, upbeat original instrumental beat. No lyrics, no talking.

The dye stays on the hair and in the bowl: never on the face or the skin. Anatomy rules: one head, two hands, five fingers on each hand. Exactly one person: no hairdresser, no other hands. No text, no captions, no logos, no watermark, no brand names on the bowl, the brush or the flat iron.

A funny, photorealistic electric shock prank clip, vertical 9:16, like a real phone video.

The person is the one in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles), and the clothes from [Image 1], so their friends recognize them at once. Before the spark the hair is as in [Image 1].
The pet is the animal in [Image 2], in every frame: the same species and breed, face, eyes, ears, muzzle, fur colors, fur length and markings as in [Image 2], with the natural body volume and proportions of that animal: never flattened, squashed, stretched or merged with anything. A real animal with no clothes; it never turns into a person and the person never gets fur. If [Image 1] shows the animal and [Image 2] the person, swap the roles. The same person and the same pet from the first frame to the last; never morph.

The place: a cozy home workshop in soft daylight: a wooden workbench with a few screwdrivers, pliers and loose screws on it, a pegboard of tools and a shelf behind.

Shot 1, 0-2 s: a low camera at the edge of the bench, level with the faces. Behind it lean the person on the LEFT and the pet on the RIGHT, its front paws on the bench, side by side, frowning in deep concentration. The one on the LEFT pinches a loose RED wire, the one on the RIGHT holds a loose BLUE wire; they slowly bring the bare ends together.
Shot 2, 2-4.5 s: an extreme close-up of the two bare wire ends, red and blue, shiny copper strands, the workshop blurred behind; they creep closer, then TOUCH: a small bright blue-white spark and a crackle.
Shot 3, 4.5-5 s: a big puff of dark grey cartoon smoke fills the whole frame, like in an old comic.
Shot 4, 5-10 s: back to the framing of shot 1 as the smoke clears. Both sit where they were, frozen, still holding their wires, and they are fine: grey soot smudges on the cheeks and noses, a thin curl of smoke above their heads. The person's hair stands straight up in a huge frizzy, spiky crown; the pet's fur is puffed out and standing on end all over. Wide eyes, mouths open in shock. They slowly turn and look at each other, blink, then both burst out laughing: the pet opens its mouth in a big happy grin, wags its tail and gives one quick shake that puffs a little soot out of its fur. The faces stay large, sharp and turned to the camera.

Exactly one person and one animal. Harmless cartoon slapstick: nobody is hurt, no burns, no wounds, no pain, no fire on anyone; the soot and the wild hair are the whole joke.

Audio: a quiet workshop, a sharp electric crackle and zap at the spark, a soft comic poof, a beat of stunned silence, then the laughter. No music, no words.

Avoid: a wall socket, a plug, a power strip or any device the wires lead into; flames; injuries; faces changing or blending; extra people, animals, limbs or paws; morphing; text, subtitles, logos, watermarks.
```

Nighttime exterior of a modern apartment building. Start very far away, showing most of the building facade with many windows. Only one apartment window is warmly illuminated and subtly attracts attention. All the other windows are dark.

The camera begins a slow, smooth, cinematic physical push-in through the night air toward that specific illuminated window. The movement must feel like a real camera physically traveling toward the building: NOT a digital zoom, NOT a sudden crop, NOT a transition. The apartment gradually becomes larger and clearer as the camera approaches.

Through the window, 2 friends are naturally spending time together and casually using / eating / interacting with the exact PRODUCT from the uploaded product reference. The camera continues slowly approaching until the people and product are clearly visible through the window.

Then ONE person notices the camera.

He does NOT smile. He is NOT surprised or scared. He does NOT immediately close the curtains.

He maintains uncomfortable direct eye contact with the camera for approximately 2 seconds.

Then, still visibly annoyed, he slowly puts down what he is holding, calmly stands up from the table and walks naturally toward the window while maintaining eye contact with the camera.

No rushing. No sudden movements.

When he reaches the window, he pauses briefly and gives the camera one final irritated stare.

Only then, he grabs both curtains and slowly pulls them together from both sides until the window is completely covered.

The curtains physically block the camera’s view of the apartment. The closed curtains stay warmly lit from inside.

Centered logo, premium minimal presentation. Do not redesign, rewrite or distort the logo. Do not substitute any other version of the brand's logo.

As soon as the curtain closes, the logo appears, but the curtain remains softly visible in the background.

6-8.5 s: the cut to under the basket: the dunker gathers the ball, takes two long steps, rises over a late defender and slams the ball through the rim with one hand, hanging on the rim for a moment; the net snaps.
8.5-12 s: the dunker lets go of the rim, loses balance in the air and falls flat on their back on the hardwood with a soft thud, arms spread out, unhurt; then they lift their head toward the camera, the face large and sharp, and burst out laughing while the players and the crowd go wild.

Audio: sneakers squeaking, the ball bouncing on the hardwood, players shouting, the crowd going "ohhh" at the crossover, a loud rim clang, a thud on the floor, then laughter and a roaring crowd. No music, no lyrics, no commentary.

The fall is slapstick: no injury, no blood, no pain on the face. Exactly one ball. No league, team, college or arena logos, no team names, no sponsor boards with words, no scoreboard text, no captions, no watermark. The main priority is the closest possible match to [Video 1], with a complete and realistic replacement of the two people and the vehicle by the objects from [Image 1], [Image 2] and [Image 3].

```text

Recreate the reference video [Video 1] shot for shot: the same underground parking garage, the same red sports car parked behind, the same 2 of hearts and 4 of hearts that fly at the lens, the same handheld phone camera, the same framing and timing. The ONLY change: the woman with the blonde bob is now the person in [Image 1].

The one who walks toward the camera = the person in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] - face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles exactly as in the photo. The moves, matching the reference second by second:
0-2.5 s: walks toward the camera through the garage.
2.5-4.2 s: holds up the 2 and the 4 of hearts close to the lens and flicks both cards at it.
4.2-7 s: the cards fly past the lens, the red sports car is now parked behind, a confident look into the camera.
7-10 s: turns to the car, points at it with the thumb and walks out of the frame.

Anatomy rules: one head only, on their own shoulders. Two arms and two hands. No badges, emblems or lettering on the car, no readable number plates, no text, no logos, no watermark, no extra people.
```

### Prompt 1

```text
Usa mi foto subida como base. Crea una imagen muy detallada y vívida de una mujer maquillada como una Catrina o una figura asociada al Día de Muertos mexicano, ambientada con una iluminación cálida. La mujer tiene la cara maquillada en el estilo tradicional de calavera de azúcar (calavera). El área de los ojos está rodeada por grandes círculos de color naranja brillante u oro pálido, con un delineado oscuro. Hay detalles florales o de encaje sobre las cejas y en la parte superior de la frente. En el centro de la frente, tiene un adorno que parece una joya roja y dorada, con patrones de pétalos que se extienden hacia las sienes. La nariz está maquillada de negro en forma de un triángulo invertido, imitando la cavidad nasal de un cráneo. Los labios son de un color naranja suave o terracota. Alrededor de la boca, tiene dibujados los dientes de la calavera con líneas finas y curvas negras, que se extienden en una sonrisa elaborada. En las mejillas y la barbilla, hay detalles ornamentales de color dorado, que simulan filigranas o joyas. Los ojos de la mujer son de un color verde o avellana y miran directamente al frente con una expresión seria e intensa. El Tocado y Adornos: Lleva un impresionante tocado o corona en la cabeza. El elemento central del tocado es un cráneo humano (calavera) de color blanco o hueso, ubicado en la parte superior de la cabeza. Alrededor de la calavera y la cabeza, hay una abundancia de rosas de color naranja intenso o cempasúchil, que enmarcan su rostro. Detrás de las flores, hay una estructura dorada que irradia hacia arriba, como una aureola o resplandor con puntas afiladas. Vestimenta y Joyería: Lleva múltiples collares, incluyendo varias vueltas de perlas blancas y varias cadenas de oro grueso con diseños intrincados. Viste una prenda en la parte superior que tiene detalles de encaje blanco o bordado en los hombros. Lleva unos pendientes colgantes grandes y elaborados de color dorado, posiblemente con perlas o gemas colgantes. El fondo está desenfocado (bokeh), pero se aprecian varias velas encendidas que proyectan una luz cálida, dorada y difusa, lo que contribuye a una atmósfera mística y ceremonial, muy acorde con la temática. No cambies mi rostro. Formato vertical 9:16. Una sola persona. Sin texto, sin logotipos, sin marca de agua, sin marcos ni superposiciones.
```

Esta imagen es un retrato impactante de un hombre cuyo maquillaje está inspirado en las tradiciones del Día de Muertos, específicamente la Calavera Catrina. Descripción Detallada El Maquillaje (Calavera/Cráneo de Azúcar): El rostro del hombre está pintado para parecerse a un cráneo estilizado. Las cuencas de los ojos están dramáticamente agrandadas y pintadas de un vibrante naranja con intrincados detalles en oro, diamantes, perlas y blanco alrededor de los bordes, sugiriendo pétalos de cempasúchil. La nariz está pintada de negro en forma de corazón invertido o nariz de cráneo. El color natural de la piel sirve como base para el resto de su rostro, contrastando con el color oscuro de sus labios. Intrincados diseños de filigrana en oro y blanco adornan su frente, mejillas y barbilla, imitando encaje delicado o joyería. Pequeños diseños dorados en forma de mariposa están pintados en sus mejillas. Atuendo: Chaquetilla: Una chaqueta corta y ajustada. Camisa: Una camisa formal de manga larga debajo. Chaleco: Un chaleco a juego. Moño: Una corbata de lazo de ala ancha. Fondo y Ambiente: El fondo es suave, oscuro y ligeramente difuminado, sugiriendo un entorno interior, quizás histórico o con poca luz. Dos velas blancas encendidas son visibles en el lado izquierdo, añadiendo un cálido y suave resplandor a la composición general y resaltando la naturaleza dramática de su retrato. La iluminación está enfocada en su rostro, haciendo que los acentos dorados y los colores ricos realmente brillen. El ambiente general es de una belleza majestuosa, solemne y reverencia cultural. Formato vertical 9:16. Una sola persona. Sin texto, sin logotipos, sin marca de agua, sin marcos ni superposiciones.

Su rostro, con la piel pálida, presenta un maquillaje elaborado con cuencas oculares oscuras, intrincados patrones florales en la frente y mejillas, y una sutil delineación de los dientes. Ella esta de perfil y su mirada está directamente dirigida al espectador, con una expresión serena y enigmática. Lleva un gran sombrero de ala ancha adornado con múltiples girasoles de color amarillo brillante, que enmarcan su rostro y se extienden por el ala. Su vestimenta consiste en un atuendo de manga larga, predominantemente oscuro (posiblemente negro), decorado con motivos florales de girasoles en tonos amarillos, que se asemejan a los girasoles del sombrero y a los cempasúchiles del campo. Los detalles del vestuario sugieren un estilo tradicional, quizás con elementos bordados. El cabello de la mujer es largo y ondulado, cayendo sobre sus hombros. El entorno es un campo abierto, donde abundan las flores de cempasúchil en primer plano y se extienden hasta el horizonte. El fondo es ligeramente borroso, mostrando árboles y vegetación con un matiz otoñal o crepuscular, en tonos apagados de naranja, amarillo y verde oliva, bajo un cielo nublado o difuso. La iluminación es suave y difusa, con un ligero toque dramático que resalta el maquillaje y los colores vibrantes del sombrero y las flores, creando una atmósfera melancólica pero hermosa, muy acorde con la temática del Día de Muertos. La composición enfoca a la mujer en el tercio medio de la imagen, con el campo floral ocupando gran parte del encuadre. RESPETA MIS RASGOS FISICOS Y NO ME CAMBIES LA CARA Formato vertical 9:16. The lead is the person in [Image 1], in every frame: copy the exact face from [Image 1] (the face shape, eye color and eye shape, eyebrows, nose, mouth, skin tone, age, hair color, hair length and hairstyle, and any beard, moustache, glasses or freckles), so their friends recognize them at once. The lead wears a clean all-white outfit and stands perfectly still in the exact center of wide grey stone steps in front of a large building, looking straight into the camera for the whole clip. Overcast daylight, slight film grain.

The camera starts at a medium shot of the lead, from the waist up, with the crowd behind, and slowly pushes in. They repeat the bow once more and freeze. Only the crowd moves; the lead never moves and keeps the same face from the first frame to the last.

Audio: an original dark, heavy electronic beat with deep drums. No cigarettes, no text, no logos.

The left performer leans toward the microphone and raps first with loose hand gestures while the right performer nods to the beat and reacts. Then the right performer takes the lead while the left one hypes them up. The timing feels natural and conversational, never mirrored.
